  Summary:
  ## 1. Primary Request and Intent

  The user requested a comprehensive refactoring of the "Generate Image"
  functionality to align with the existing TTS (Text-to-Speech) and STT
  (Speech-to-Text) user experience patterns. The specific goals were:

  - **Persistent History within Dialog**: Implement a history list for generated
  images directly within the "Generate Image" dialog, persisting across dialog
  openings using localStorage (similar to TTS generated audio history)
  - **Seamless Chat Integration**: Enable generated images to be inserted as new
  messages or appended to existing pending messages in the main chat history
  - **Enhanced Image Interaction**: Support comprehensive interaction options
  including downloadable, expandable, and deletable images within the dialog
  history
  - **Consistent Architecture**: Follow the same state management and UI patterns
   established by the TTS/STT features

  The user provided a detailed analysis showing that the current image generation
   feature lacked:
  - Dialog state management in the model
  - Persistent history (images lost on reload)
  - Chat integration
  - Command processing integration
  - Consistent state management patterns

  ## 2. Key Technical Concepts

  - **React Functional Components** with Material-UI styling and TypeScript
  interfaces
  - **Effector State Management** with stores, events, and effects for
  predictable state updates
  - **localStorage Persistence** for maintaining generated image history across
  sessions
  - **Multimodal Message Handling** with MessageContentPart arrays including
  GeneratedImageContentPart
  - **Single Pending Message Architecture** with $activePendingMultimodalMessage
  store
  - **Content-Order-Based Rendering** for consistent attachment display
  - **State Synchronization** between multiple Effector stores
  - **Dialog State Management** following TTS/STT patterns with $isDialogOpen
  stores
  - **Blob URL Management** for proper memory cleanup of generated images
  - **Combined State Stores** for unified component consumption
  - **Event-Driven Architecture** connecting image generation to chat integration

  ### `/Users/doasync/Projects/chat-ui/src/features/image-generation/model.ts`
  **Purpose**: Core state management refactoring to follow TTS/STT patterns
  **Changes Made**: Complete architectural overhaul including:

  ```typescript
  // Added dialog state management
  export const $isDialogOpen = imageGenerationDomain.store<boolean>(
    false,
    { name: "isDialogOpen" }
  );

  // Added prompt management
  export const $imagePrompt = imageGenerationDomain.store<string>(
    "",
    { name: "imagePrompt" }
  );

  // New events following TTS/STT pattern
  export const dialogOpened = imageGenerationDomain.event<void>("dialogOpened");
  export const dialogClosed = imageGenerationDomain.event<void>("dialogClosed");
  export const promptChanged =
  imageGenerationDomain.event<string>("promptChanged");
  export const sendImageToChat =
  imageGenerationDomain.event<string>("sendImageToChat");

  // localStorage persistence effects
  export const loadGeneratedImagesFx = imageGenerationDomain.effect<void,
  GeneratedImage[]>({
    name: "loadGeneratedImagesFx",
    handler: async () => {
      try {
        const stored = localStorage.getItem('generatedImagesHistory');
        if (stored) {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(stored);
          if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
            return parsed.filter(item =>
              item &&
              typeof item === 'object' &&
              typeof item.id === 'string' &&
              typeof item.prompt === 'string' &&
              typeof item.model === 'string' &&
              typeof item.timestamp === 'number'
            );
          }
        }
        return [];
      } catch (error) {
        console.warn('Failed to load generated images from localStorage:',
  error);
        return [];
      }
    },
  });

  // Combined state for easy consumption
  export const $imageGenerationState = combine({
    isDialogOpen: $isDialogOpen,
    prompt: $imagePrompt,
    isGenerating: $isGeneratingImage,
    error: $imageGenerationError,
    selectedModel: $selectedImageGenModel,
    modelInfo: $selectedImageGenModelInfo,
    settings: $imageGenerationSettings,
    availableModels: $availableImageGenModels,
    generatedImages: $generatedImages,
    supportsEditing: $currentModelSupportsEditing,
    supportsVariations: $currentModelSupportsVariations,
    supportedSizes: $currentModelSupportedSizes,
    supportedQualities: $currentModelSupportedQualities,
    supportedStyles: $currentModelSupportedStyles,
  });
  ```

  ### `/Users/doasync/Projects/chat-ui/src/features/chat/model.ts`
  **Purpose**: Integration point for connecting image generation to chat system
  **Changes Made**: Added event handling for generated image integration:

  ```typescript
  // Event to add generated image to chat
  export const addGeneratedImageToChat = chatDomain.event<{
    imageId: string;
    url?: string;
    b64_json?: string;
    prompt: string;
    model: string;
    parameters?: {
      size?: string;
      quality?: string;
      style?: string;
      n?: number;
    };
  }>("addGeneratedImageToChat");

  // Handle adding generated image to chat
  $activePendingMultimodalMessage.on(addGeneratedImageToChat, (currentPending,
  imageData) => {
    // Create generated image content part
    const generatedImagePart: GeneratedImageContentPart = {
      type: "generated_image",
      generated_image: {
        url: imageData.url,
        b64_json: imageData.b64_json,
        prompt: imageData.prompt,
        model: imageData.model,
        parameters: imageData.parameters,
      },
    };

    // Create or update pending message
    if (!currentPending) {
      return {
        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
        role: "user" as const,
        content: [generatedImagePart],
        timestamp: Date.now(),
        status: "pending" as const,
        attachments: [],
      };
    } else {
      const existingContent = Array.isArray(currentPending.content)
        ? currentPending.content
        : currentPending.content
          ? [{ type: "text" as const, text: currentPending.content }]
          : [];

      return {
        ...currentPending,
        content: [...existingContent, generatedImagePart],
        timestamp: Date.now(),
      };
    }
  });

  // Connect sendImageToChat to addGeneratedImageToChat
  sample({
    clock: sendImageToChat,
    source: $generatedImages,
    fn: (generatedImages, imageId) => {
      const image = generatedImages.find(img => img.id === imageId);
      if (!image) {
        throw new Error(`Generated image with ID ${imageId} not found`);
      }

      return {
        imageId: image.id,
        url: image.url,
        b64_json: image.b64_json,
        prompt: image.prompt,
        model: image.model,
        parameters: image.parameters,
      };
    },
    target: addGeneratedImageToChat,
  });
  ```

  ### `/Users/doasync/Projects/chat-ui/src/components/ImageGenerationDialog.tsx`

  // Updated to use combined state
  const [state, apiKey] = useUnit([$imageGenerationState, $apiKey]);

  const handleSendToChat = (imageId: string) => {
    sendImageToChat(imageId);
    dialogClosed();
  };

  const handleRemoveImage = (imageId: string) => {
    removeGeneratedImage(imageId);
  };

  // New history display with action buttons
  {state.generatedImages.length > 0 && (
    <Box sx={{ mt: 3 }}>
      <Typography variant="h6" gutterBottom>
        Generated Images ({state.generatedImages.length})
      </Typography>

          display: 'grid',

          gap: 2,

          overflowY: 'auto',

        {state.generatedImages
          .sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp)
          .map((image) => (
            <Card key={image.id}>
              {/* Image display with metadata */}
              <CardActions>
                <Button
                  startIcon={<SendIcon />}
                  onClick={() => handleSendToChat(image.id)}
                  variant="outlined"
                >
                  Send to Chat
                </Button>
                <IconButton
                  onClick={() => handleRemoveImage(image.id)}
                  color="error"
                >
                  <DeleteIcon />
                </IconButton>
              </CardActions>
            </Card>
          ))}
      </Box>
    </Box>
  )}

> I have carefully analyzed your report on the current blocking nature of image
  generation. This is a critical user experience and performance bottleneck. I
  have refined your draft prompt into a comprehensive debugging and resolution
  request below.

  Does this revised prompt accurately capture the desired parallel generation
  behavior and guide our approach for resolution? If so, please copy it and send
  it back to me as the official task.

  ---

  ### **Improved Prompt (Generated by Roo, for you to use)**

  **Subject: Feature Enhancement: Implement Parallel Image Generation with
  Per-Placeholder Progress**

  Hello Roo,

  The current "Generate Image" functionality suffers from a significant user
  experience flaw: image generation is a blocking operation. The UI is locked,
  and the progress indicator is tied to the "Generate" button, preventing users
  from initiating subsequent generations while one is in progress. This leads to
  a bottleneck and a frustrating wait.

  **1. Problem Description:**

  *   **Blocking Operation:** Users are blocked from initiating a new image
  generation request while a previous one is still being processed.
  *   **Global Progress Indicator:** The progress indicator is tied to the
  "Generate" button or a global dialog state, not directly to the image
  placeholder. This provides vague feedback and prevents granular tracking of
  multiple generations.
  *   **Lack of Parallelism:** Only one image can be generated at a time, forcing
   sequential execution of requests.

  **2. Desired Functionality: Non-Blocking, Parallel Image Generation**

  The goal is to enable users to initiate multiple image generation requests
  concurrently, with clear, individual progress feedback for each generated
  image.

  **2.1. Parallel Generation Capability:**
      *   **Goal:** Allow users to click the "Generate" button multiple times in
  rapid succession, triggering new image generation requests without waiting for
  previous ones to complete.
      *   **Behavior:** Each click of the "Generate" button should:
          1.  Immediately create a new placeholder item in the image generation
  history list.
          2.  Assign a unique ID to this new generation request/placeholder.
          3.  Initiate an asynchronous image generation process for this request.

  **2.2. Per-Placeholder Progress Indication:**
      *   **Goal:** Provide visual feedback on the generation status directly on
  each individual image placeholder, rather than on the "Generate" button.
      *   **Behavior:**
          1.  When a generation request is initiated, its corresponding
  placeholder should appear in a "pending" or "loading" state (e.g., displaying a
   spinner, a grey box, or a text indicator like "Generating...").
          2.  As the image data (or progress updates, if the API supports partial
   streams) becomes available, the placeholder should update to reflect progress
  or eventually display the completed image.
          3.  Error states should also be clearly indicated on the individual
  placeholder if a generation fails.

  **2.3. Dynamic Placeholder Updates:**
      *   **Goal:** When a specific generation request completes, its
  corresponding placeholder in the UI should seamlessly transition from the
  "pending" state to displaying the final generated image.
      *   **Behavior:** The finished image, along with its metadata, should
  replace the placeholder.

  **3. Debugging and Resolution Strategy:**

  This will require a fundamental shift in how image generation state is managed,
   moving from a single "is generating" boolean to a more granular,
  request-specific tracking system.

  **3.1. Redefine Generation State Management:**
      *   **Target:** `src/features/image-generation/model.ts` and
  `src/features/image-generation/types.ts`.
      *   **Action:**
          *   The `$isGeneratingImage` store should reflect whether *any*
  generation is active, but individual generation status needs to be tracked.
          *   Modify the `GeneratedImage` interface or create a new
  `GenerationInProgress` interface to include a `status` field (e.g., `pending`,
  `generating`, `completed`, `error`) and potentially a `progress` value for each
   item.
          *   Maintain a list/map of currently active generation requests (e.g.,
  `$activeGenerations: Map<string, GenerationInProgress>`).

  **3.2. Adapt `generateImageFx` and its Handling:**
      *   **Target:** `src/features/image-generation/model.ts`.
      *   **Action:**
          *   When `generateImageFx` is triggered, it should:
              1.  Immediately create a new unique ID for the request.
              2.  Add a new entry to the `$activeGenerations` map with `status:
  'pending'` and its ID.
              3.  Modify the `generateImageFx.doneData`, `failData`, `finally`
  handlers to update the `status` of the *specific* entry in `$activeGenerations`
   using its unique ID.
          *   Consider adding an `onStart` or similar callback to
  `generateImageFx` to update the status when the API call is actually
  dispatched.

  **3.3. Update Image Generation Dialog UI:**
      *   **Target:** `src/components/ImageGenerationDialog.tsx`.
      *   **Action:**
          *   Modify the history display to iterate through `$generatedImages`
  (which now might also include `pending` or `error` states).
          *   For each item, render either the final image or a placeholder with
  a progress indicator/status message, based on its `status` field.
          *   The "Generate" button should remain enabled even if other
  generations are active.

  **4. Deliverables:**

  *   Ability to initiate multiple image generation requests simultaneously.
  *   Visual progress and status for each individual generated image placeholder.
  *   A non-blocking user experience for image generation.

  ---



⏺ Yes, this revised prompt accurately captures the desired parallel generation
  behavior and provides an excellent roadmap for resolution. The analysis is

  ---
  Feature Enhancement: Implement Parallel Image Generation with Per-Placeholder
  Progress

  Hello Roo,

  The current "Generate Image" functionality suffers from a significant user
  experience flaw: image generation is a blocking operation. The UI is locked,
  and the progress indicator is tied to the "Generate" button, preventing users
  from initiating subsequent generations while one is in progress. This leads to
  a bottleneck and a frustrating wait.

  1. Problem Description:

  - Blocking Operation: Users are blocked from initiating a new image generation
  request while a previous one is still being processed.
  - Global Progress Indicator: The progress indicator is tied to the "Generate"
  button or a global dialog state, not directly to the image placeholder. This
  provides vague feedback and prevents granular tracking of multiple generations.
  - Lack of Parallelism: Only one image can be generated at a time, forcing
  sequential execution of requests.

  2. Desired Functionality: Non-Blocking, Parallel Image Generation

  The goal is to enable users to initiate multiple image generation requests
  concurrently, with clear, individual progress feedback for each generated
  image.

  2.1. Parallel Generation Capability:
      *   Goal: Allow users to click the "Generate" button multiple times in
  rapid succession, triggering new image generation requests without waiting for
  previous ones to complete.
      *   Behavior: Each click of the "Generate" button should:
          1.  Immediately create a new placeholder item in the image generation
  history list.
          2.  Assign a unique ID to this new generation request/placeholder.
          3.  Initiate an asynchronous image generation process for this request.

  2.2. Per-Placeholder Progress Indication:
      *   Goal: Provide visual feedback on the generation status directly on each
   individual image placeholder, rather than on the "Generate" button.
      *   Behavior:
          1.  When a generation request is initiated, its corresponding
  placeholder should appear in a "pending" or "loading" state (e.g., displaying a
   spinner, a grey box, or a text indicator like "Generating...").
          2.  As the image data (or progress updates, if the API supports partial
   streams) becomes available, the placeholder should update to reflect progress
  or eventually display the completed image.
          3.  Error states should also be clearly indicated on the individual
  placeholder if a generation fails.

  2.3. Dynamic Placeholder Updates:
      *   Goal: When a specific generation request completes, its corresponding
  placeholder in the UI should seamlessly transition from the "pending" state to
  displaying the final generated image.
      *   Behavior: The finished image, along with its metadata, should replace
  the placeholder.

  3. Debugging and Resolution Strategy:

  This will require a fundamental shift in how image generation state is managed,
   moving from a single "is generating" boolean to a more granular,
  request-specific tracking system.

  3.1. Redefine Generation State Management:
      *   Target: src/features/image-generation/model.ts and
  src/features/image-generation/types.ts.
      *   Action:
          *   The $isGeneratingImage store should reflect whether any generation
  is active, but individual generation status needs to be tracked.
          *   Modify the GeneratedImage interface or create a new
  GenerationInProgress interface to include a status field (e.g., pending,
  generating, completed, error) and potentially a progress value for each item.
          *   Maintain a list/map of currently active generation requests (e.g.,
  $activeGenerations: Map<string, GenerationInProgress>).

  3.2. Adapt generateImageFx and its Handling:
      *   Target: src/features/image-generation/model.ts.
      *   Action:
          *   When generateImageFx is triggered, it should:
              1.  Immediately create a new unique ID for the request.
              2.  Add a new entry to the $activeGenerations map with status:
  'pending' and its ID.
              3.  Modify the generateImageFx.doneData, failData, finally handlers
   to update the status of the specific entry in $activeGenerations using its
  unique ID.
          *   Consider adding an onStart or similar callback to generateImageFx
  to update the status when the API call is actually dispatched.

  3.3. Update Image Generation Dialog UI:
      *   Target: src/components/ImageGenerationDialog.tsx.
      *   Action:
          *   Modify the history display to iterate through $generatedImages
  (which now might also include pending or error states).
          *   For each item, render either the final image or a placeholder with
  a progress indicator/status message, based on its status field.
          *   The "Generate" button should remain enabled even if other
  generations are active.

  4. Deliverables:

  - Ability to initiate multiple image generation requests simultaneously.
  - Visual progress and status for each individual generated image placeholder.
  - A non-blocking user experience for image generation.

     browser support
         + wser support
     117 +      if (imageUrl.startsWith('data:')) {
     118 +        try {
     119 +          // Extract base64 data and convert to blob
     120 +          const base64Data = imageUrl.split(',')[1];
     121 +          const byteCharacters = atob(base64Data);
     122 +          const byteNumbers = new Array(byteCharacters.length);
     123 +          for (let i = 0; i < byteCharacters.length; i++) {
     124 +            byteNumbers[i] = byteCharacters.charCodeAt(i);
     125 +          }
     126 +          const byteArray = new Uint8Array(byteNumbers);
     127 +          const blob = new Blob([byteArray], { type: 'image/png' });
     128 +          const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
     129 +
     130 +          // Open blob URL and clean up after a delay
     131 +          const newWindow = window.open(blobUrl, '_blank');
     132 +          if (newWindow) {
     133 +            // Clean up blob URL after window loads or after a delay
     134 +            setTimeout(() => {
     135 +              URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl);
     136 +            }, 1000);
     137 +          }
     138 +        } catch (error) {
     139 +          console.error('Failed to convert data URL to blob:', error);
     140 +          // Fallback to original method
     141 +          window.open(imageUrl, "_blank");
     142 +        }
     143 +      } else {
     144 +        // Regular URL, open directly

> **Subject: Feature Enhancement: Implement Parallel Image Generation with
  Per-Placeholder Progress**

  The current "Generate Image" functionality suffers from a significant user
  experience flaw: image generation is a blocking operation. The UI is locked,
  and the progress indicator is tied to the "Generate" button, preventing users
  from initiating subsequent generations while one is in progress. This leads to
  a bottleneck and a frustrating wait.

  **1. Problem Description:**

  *   **Blocking Operation:** Users are blocked from initiating a new image
  generation request while a previous one is still being processed.
  *   **Global Progress Indicator:** The progress indicator is tied to the
  "Generate" button or a global dialog state, not directly to the image
  placeholder. This provides vague feedback and prevents granular tracking of
  multiple generations.
  *   **Lack of Parallelism:** Only one image can be generated at a time, forcing
   sequential execution of requests.

  **2. Desired Functionality: Non-Blocking, Parallel Image Generation**

  The goal is to enable users to initiate multiple image generation requests
  concurrently, with clear, individual progress feedback for each generated
  image.

  **2.1. Parallel Generation Capability:**
      *   **Goal:** Allow users to click the "Generate" button multiple times in
  rapid succession, triggering new image generation requests without waiting for
  previous ones to complete.
      *   **Behavior:** Each click of the "Generate" button should:
          1.  Immediately create a new placeholder item in the image generation
  history list.
          2.  Assign a unique ID to this new generation request/placeholder.
          3.  Initiate an asynchronous image generation process for this request.

  **2.2. Per-Placeholder Progress Indication:**
      *   **Goal:** Provide visual feedback on the generation status directly on
  each individual image placeholder, rather than on the "Generate" button.
      *   **Behavior:**
          1.  When a generation request is initiated, its corresponding
  placeholder should appear in a "pending" or "loading" state (e.g., displaying ,
   a horizontal progress bar, or a text indicator like "Generating...").
          2.  As the image data (or progress updates, if the API supports partial
   streams) becomes available, the placeholder should update to reflect progress
  or eventually display the completed image.
          3.  Error states should also be clearly indicated on the individual
  placeholder if a generation fails.

  **2.3. Dynamic Placeholder Updates:**
      *   **Goal:** When a specific generation request completes, its
  corresponding placeholder in the UI should seamlessly transition from the
  "pending" state to displaying the final generated image.
      *   **Behavior:** The finished image, along with its metadata, should
  replace the placeholder.

  **3. Debugging and Resolution Strategy:**

  This will require a fundamental shift in how image generation state is managed,
   moving from a single "is generating" boolean to a more granular,
  request-specific tracking system.

  **3.1. Redefine Generation State Management:**
      *   **Target:** `src/features/image-generation/model.ts` and
  `src/features/image-generation/types.ts`.
      *   **Action:**
          *   The `$isGeneratingImage` store should reflect whether *any*
  generation is active, but individual generation status needs to be tracked.
          *   Modify the `GeneratedImage` interface or create a new
  `GenerationInProgress` interface to include a `status` field (e.g., `pending`,
  `generating`, `completed`, `error`) and potentially a `progress` value for each
   item.
          *   Maintain a list/map of currently active generation requests (e.g.,
  `$activeGenerations: Map<string, GenerationInProgress>`).

  **3.2. Adapt `generateImageFx` and its Handling:**
      *   **Target:** `src/features/image-generation/model.ts`.
      *   **Action:**
          *   When `generateImageFx` is triggered, it should:
              1.  Immediately create a new unique ID for the request.
              2.  Add a new entry to the `$activeGenerations` map with `status:
  'pending'` and its ID.
              3.  Modify the `generateImageFx.doneData`, `failData`, `finally`
  handlers to update the `status` of the *specific* entry in `$activeGenerations`
   using its unique ID.
          *   Consider adding an `onStart` or similar callback to
  `generateImageFx` to update the status when the API call is actually
  dispatched.

  **3.3. Update Image Generation Dialog UI:**
      *   **Target:** `src/components/ImageGenerationDialog.tsx`.
      *   **Action:**
          *   Modify the history display to iterate through `$generatedImages`
  (which now might also include `pending` or `error` states).
          *   For each item, render either the final image or a placeholder with
  a progress indicator/status message, based on its `status` field.
          *   The "Generate" button should remain enabled even if other
  generations are active.

  **4. Deliverables:**

  *   Ability to initiate multiple image generation requests simultaneously.
  *   Visual progress and status for each individual generated image placeholder.
  *   A non-blocking user experience for image generation.

⏺ Perfect! I have successfully implemented parallel image generation with
  per-placeholder progress tracking. Here's a summary of what was accomplished:

  Implementation Summary: Parallel Image Generation

  ✅ Completed Features:

  1. Extended Type System:
    - Added ImageGenerationStatus type with values: 'pending' | 'generating' |
  'completed' | 'error'
    - Enhanced GeneratedImage interface with status, error, and progress fields
  2. Per-Request Tracking:
    - Implemented immediate placeholder creation when generation starts
    - Added $activeGenerationRequests store for tracking request IDs
    - Created event-driven status updates (imageGenerationStarted,
  imageGenerationUpdated, imageGenerationCompleted)
  3. Non-Blocking UI:
    - Generate button remains enabled during generation, changing text to
  "Generate Another Image"
    - All form controls stay enabled (model selection, prompt, settings)
    - Dialog can be closed even during active generation
    - Multiple concurrent requests are supported
  4. Per-Placeholder Progress Indicators:
    - Pending state: Shows circular progress with "Queued..." text
    - Generating state: Shows circular progress with "Generating..." text and
  optional progress bar
    - Error state: Shows error message with red background
    - Completed state: Shows final image with full interaction capabilities
  5. Status-Based Actions:
    - Completed images: Full action set (expand, download, send to chat, delete)
    - Pending/Generating images: Limited to cancel/delete action with status text
    - Error images: Show error details with delete option
  6. Enhanced User Experience:
    - Immediate feedback: Placeholders appear instantly when Generate is clicked
    - Visual distinction: Different states are clearly indicated with colors and
  icons
    - Parallel workflow: Users can initiate multiple generations without waiting
    - Progress transparency: Each image shows its individual generation status

  🔧 Technical Implementation:

  - State Flow: generateImage → imageGenerationStarted → generateImageFx →
  imageGenerationCompleted/Updated
  - Request Tracking: Uses request IDs to map async effects to specific
  placeholders
  - UI Rendering: Conditional rendering based on image.status with appropriate
  progress indicators
  - Storage Migration: Existing localStorage data automatically migrated with
  default 'completed' status

  📋 Deliverables Achieved:

  ✅ Ability to initiate multiple image generation requests simultaneously✅
  Visual progress and status for each individual generated image placeholder✅
  Non-blocking user experience for image generation

  The implementation successfully transforms the blocking, single-request image
  generation into a modern, parallel workflow that provides clear feedback and
  allows users to efficiently manage multiple concurrent generations.

  spinning card issue:

  🔧 Problem Resolution: Cards Still Spinning After Successful Generation

  Root Cause:

  The issue was that the requestId wasn't being properly tracked through the
  async effect completion, so when imageGenerationCompleted was triggered, the
  placeholder couldn't be matched with the completed image data.

  Solution Implemented:

  1. Modified Effect Return Type:
  // Before: effect returned just ImageGenerationResponse
  // After: effect returns { requestId: string; response: ImageGenerationResponse
   }
  2. Simplified Request Tracking:
    - Removed the complex $activeGenerationRequests mapping approach
    - Effect now returns both the requestId and the response data
    - Completion handler directly accesses the requestId from the effect result
  3. Updated Sample Connections:
  // Generation completion now gets requestId directly
  sample({
    clock: generateImageFx.doneData,
    fn: ({ requestId, response }) => ({
      id: requestId,
      url: response.data[0]?.url,
      b64_json: response.data[0]?.b64_json,
    }),
    target: imageGenerationCompleted,
  });
  4. Added Debug Logging:
    - Console logs to track placeholder creation, completion, and image updates
    - This will help verify the fix is working correctly

  Expected Behavior After Fix:

  1. Generate button clicked → Placeholder appears with "Queued..." status
  2. API call starts → Status changes to "Generating..." with spinner
  3. API responds successfully → Status changes to "completed" and shows actual
  image
  4. Card no longer spins → Full interaction capabilities enabled (expand,
  download, send to chat)

  The debugging logs will show in the browser console when testing to confirm
  each step is working properly. The key fix ensures that when the effect
  completes successfully, the correct placeholder is identified and updated with
  the actual image data.

⏺ Perfect! Now the issue with failed requests should be resolved. Here's a
  summary of the fix:

  🔧 Fix for Failed Requests Still Showing Loading State

  Problem:

  When image generation requests failed, the placeholders remained in
  "generating" state with spinning indicators instead of showing the error state
  with detailed error messages.

  Root Cause:

  The error handling in generateImageFx.failData couldn't map failed requests
  back to their specific placeholders because regular Error objects don't contain
   the requestId.

  Solution Implemented:

  1. Created Custom Error Class:
  class ImageGenerationError extends Error {
    constructor(message: string, public requestId: string) {
      super(message);
      this.name = 'ImageGenerationError';
    }
  }
  2. Updated All Error Throws:
    - All validation errors now use ImageGenerationError with the requestId
    - HTTP errors also include the requestId for proper tracking
    - Validation errors, API errors, and network errors all preserve request
  context
  3. Enhanced Error Handling:
  sample({
    clock: generateImageFx.failData,
    fn: (error) => {
      // Extract requestId from custom error
      const requestId = error instanceof ImageGenerationError
        ? error.requestId
        : 'error-unknown';
      return {
        id: requestId,
        status: 'error' as ImageGenerationStatus,
        error: error.message,
      };
    },
    target: imageGenerationUpdated,
  });

  Expected Behavior After Fix:

  ✅ Successful generation: pending → generating → completed (shows image)✅
  Failed generation: pending → generating → error (shows error message)

  Error placeholders now show:
  - Red background with error styling
  - "Generation Failed" message
  - Detailed error message from the API/validation
  - Delete button only (no expand/download actions)
  - Proper status text: "Failed to generate"

  The fix ensures that when any type of error occurs during image generation
  (validation errors, API errors, network failures), the specific placeholder
  that initiated the request will be properly updated to show the error state
  instead of continuing to spin indefinitely.
